import type { Session } from "./contract.ts";

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#154-supplied-client-sessions — validate before effects and retain a private primitive snapshot.
export function suppliedSession(value: unknown, constructor: string): Session | undefined {
  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  const invalid = (field: string): never => {
    throw new TypeError(`${constructor}: E_SESSION: session.${field} is missing or invalid. Pass the complete Session returned by guest(), login(), signup() or your service's session minting; One-line token-only fix: createClient({ url, token }).`);
  };
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) invalid("object");
  const input = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const principal = input.principal, kind = input.kind, token = input.token;
  const expiresAt = input.expiresAt, scope = input.scope, capExpiresAt = input.capExpiresAt;
  const fields: string[] = [];
  if (typeof principal !== "string" || principal.trim().length === 0) fields.push("principal");
  if (typeof kind !== "string" || kind.trim().length === 0) fields.push("kind");
  if (typeof token !== "string" || token.trim().length === 0) fields.push("token");
  if (typeof expiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt) || expiresAt <= 0) fields.push("expiresAt (positive integer milliseconds)");
  if (scope !== undefined && scope !== "full" && scope !== "read") fields.push("scope (full or read)");
  if (capExpiresAt !== undefined && (typeof capExpiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(capExpiresAt) || capExpiresAt <= 0)) fields.push("capExpiresAt (positive integer milliseconds)");
  if (fields.length) invalid(fields.join(", session."));
  return Object.freeze({ principal, kind, token, expiresAt, ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, "scope") ? { scope } : {}), ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, "capExpiresAt") ? { capExpiresAt } : {}) }) as Session;
}

/** Legacy online credentials carry only a bearer; identity and expiry stay server facts. */
export function suppliedOnlineSession(value: unknown): { session?: Session; token?: string } {
  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
    const input = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const token = input.token;
    if (!["principal", "kind", "token", "expiresAt"].every(key => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, key))
        && typeof token === "string" && token.trim().length > 0) {
      return { token };
    }
  }
  const session = suppliedSession(value, "createClient");
  return session ? { session } : {};
}

struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
        state.advancing = false;
        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
    }
}

fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if stopping {
        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
    }
    Ok(work)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> bool {
    lock(&shared.engine).store().deployment_environment().ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging")
}
fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if shared.personas { return Ok(None); }
    if !staging(shared) { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
        Ok(service) => service,
        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
    };
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let boolean = |key: &str, default: bool| -> Result<bool, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
        match payload.get(key) {
            None => Ok(default), Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => Ok(*value),
            Some(_) => Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("{key} must be a boolean"))),
        }
    };
    let (drain, allow_held) = match boolean("drain", true).and_then(|drain| Ok((drain, boolean("allowHeld", false)?))) {
        Ok(options) => options,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    if each_boundary && !drain { return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary requires drain:true"))})); }
    let preserve_sessions = match payload.get("sessions").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        None if payload.get("sessions").is_none() => !shared.personas,
        Some("preserve") if !shared.personas => true,
        Some("expire") => false,
        _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("sessions must be preserve (staging only) or expire"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(|duration| if duration == "0ms" && drain { Ok(0) } else { crate::dev_clock::duration(duration) }) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        if let Some(principal) = &service {
            if engine.store().deployment_environment().map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&engine,principal)?;
        } else { shared.dev_authority(&engine)?; }
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = (if milliseconds == 0 { next.now() } else { next.advance(milliseconds)? }).max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — refuse a saved boundary before any clock/session effect.
        if drain && !allow_held && !shared.hold_schedules {
            crate::dev_clock_store::refuse_held(&engine, &before, now)?;
        }
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        if milliseconds > 0 {
            if !shared.personas { engine.advance_staging_clock_at(milliseconds, clock.now(), preserve_sessions) }
            else { engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()) }.map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        }
        lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = !drain;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        if !drain { return Ok(Work::default()); }
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":!drain, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => {
            let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            let after = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
            let fired: Vec<_> = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().filter(|(i, _)| {
                matches!((before.get(*i).copied().flatten(), after.get(*i).copied().flatten()), (Some(old), Some(new)) if new > old)
            }).map(|(_, schedule)| schedule.name.clone()).collect();
            let mut schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            for (index, schedule) in schedules.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if schedule["fired"] != true && !shared.hold_schedules && before.get(index).copied().flatten().is_some_and(|old| old < engine.schema().schedules[index].boundary(now)) {
                    schedule["holdCode"] = "maintenance-failed".into();
                    schedule["reason"] = "clock advanced but maintenance stopped before this due boundary was queued; inspect clock and queued jobs before continuing; do not replay the advance".into();
                }
            }
            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
            respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":!drain, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held, "reportIncomplete":true, "denied":refusal_json(&error)}))
        },
    }
}

    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
    let service=if service_route {

/bin/bash -lc "timeout 10s sed -n '1,260p' snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_maintenance.rs" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b22-dx6
 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Timing-critical maintenance and bulk catch-up share bounded engine slices.
use super::{Shared, lock, refusal_json, respond};
use serde_json::json;
use snapback4_core::RuntimeError;
use std::{sync::Arc, time::{Duration, Instant}};
use tiny_http::Request;

#[derive(Default, serde::Serialize)]
struct Work { delivery_chunks: usize, expired: u64, schedules: usize, chunks: usize, parked: usize, #[serde(skip)] fired: Vec<String> }

const WORK_TIME: Duration = Duration::from_millis(2);
const CONTENDED_TIME: Duration = Duration::from_micros(250);

// Charge consumed CPU for pacing, not time the OS already kept this thread off
// CPU. The cooperative chunk deadline still uses wall time. A failed clock
// sample falls back to the more conservative wall-time pause.
struct SliceClock { wall: Instant, cpu: Option<Duration> }
impl SliceClock {
    fn start() -> Self { Self { wall: Instant::now(), cpu: Self::cpu_time() } }
    fn cpu_time() -> Option<Duration> {
        let mut at = libc::timespec { tv_sec: 0, tv_nsec: 0 };
        if unsafe { libc::clock_gettime(libc::CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &mut at) } != 0 { return None; }
        Some(Duration::new(at.tv_sec.try_into().ok()?, at.tv_nsec.try_into().ok()?))
    }
    fn consumed(&self) -> Duration {
        self.cpu.zip(Self::cpu_time()).and_then(|(start, end)| end.checked_sub(start)).unwrap_or_else(|| self.wall.elapsed())
    }
}

// Detached readers do not take engine tickets. Sample OS load as well as the
// round-3 engine contention signal; cache the syscall for 100 ms. Load includes
// other tenants and trails short bursts, so this is a pacing hint, not admission.
fn host_contended() -> bool {
    use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};
    static SAMPLE: Mutex<(Option<Instant>, bool)> = Mutex::new((None, false));
    static CPUS: OnceLock<usize> = OnceLock::new();
    let mut sample = lock(&SAMPLE);
    if sample.0.is_none_or(|at| at.elapsed() >= Duration::from_millis(100)) {
        let mut load = 0.0;
        let cpus = *CPUS.get_or_init(|| std::thread::available_parallelism().map_or(1, |n| n.get()));
        sample.1 = unsafe { libc::getloadavg(&mut load, 1) } == 1 && load >= cpus as f64;
        sample.0 = Some(Instant::now());
    }
    sample.1
}

fn engine_work<T>(shared: &Shared, run: impl FnOnce(&mut snapback4_core::Engine, Duration) -> T) -> T {
    let mut engine = shared.engine.background();
    lock(&shared.readers).retry_missing(&engine);
    let budget = if engine.contended() || host_contended() { CONTENDED_TIME } else { WORK_TIME };
    let result = run(&mut engine, budget);
    // Publish from this guard: a second ordinary admission would both delay
    // notification and mistake maintenance for a newly arrived request.
    shared.publish_engine(&engine);
    result
}

fn phase<T: Default>(shared: &Shared, result: Result<T, RuntimeError>, drain: bool, name: &str) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
    match result {
        Err(error) if !drain => { super::adoption::background_failure(shared, name, &error); Ok(T::default()) }
        other => other,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — lifetimes are swept into the partition log; missed schedule boundaries fire once.
fn work(shared: &Shared, drain: bool, tick: u64) -> Result<Work, RuntimeError> {
    work_at(shared, drain, tick, None)
}

fn work_at(shared: &Shared, drain: bool, tick: u64, schedule_sample: Option<i64>) -> Result<Work, RuntimeError> {
    let mut work = Work::default();
    shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now())?;
    work.delivery_chunks = delivery(shared, drain)?;
    loop {
        if phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.run_view_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "view recount")?.is_none() {break;}
        work.chunks += 1;
        if !drain && work.chunks >= 500 {break;}
        std::thread::yield_now();
    }
    loop {
        let swept = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.sweep_expired_for(shared.now(), 2_000, budget)), drain, "the expiry sweep")?;
        work.expired += swept;
        if !drain || swept == 0 { break; }
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — advancing a replay clock does not release its schedule hold.
    if !shared.hold_schedules {
        let fired = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, _| {
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.2 — select advance windows before draining; admission deadlines still use the live advanced clock.
            if let Some(sample) = schedule_sample {
                let windows = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| (index, schedule.boundary(sample))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                let mut fired = Vec::new();
                for (index, boundary) in windows { fired.extend(engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, boundary)?); }
                Ok(fired)
            } else { engine.fire_schedules(shared.now()) }
        }), drain, "schedules")?;
        work.schedules = fired.len();
        for name in &fired { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: schedule {name} fired"); }
        work.fired = fired;
    }
    while drain || work.chunks < 500 {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { break; }
        let (run, logs) = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| {
            let run = engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget);
            (run, engine.take_native_logs())
        });
        for line in logs { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
        let Some(run) = phase(shared, run, drain, "job")? else { break; };
        work.chunks += 1;
        match run.state {
            snapback4_core::JobState::Done => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} done: {} chunk{}, {} row{} written", run.name, run.chunks, if run.chunks == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, run.rows_written, if run.rows_written == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
            snapback4_core::JobState::Parked => {
                work.parked += 1;
                if shared.personas { shared.refusals.job(&run); }
                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} parked after {} chunks: {}", run.name, run.chunks, run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        // FIFO engine admission hands waiting requests their turn before the
        // next chunk; yield_now alone cannot make that promise for a mutex.
    }
    if drain || tick % 5 == 0 {
        loop {
            let actions = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.requeue_expired_actions_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "the action lease sweep")?;
            let effects = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.requeue_expired_effects_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "the effect lease sweep")?;
            if actions + effects > 0 { shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); }
            if effects > 0 { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: {effects} effect lease{} expired; queued again", if effects == 1 { "" } else { "s" }); }
            if !drain || actions + effects == 0 { break; }
        }
    }
    if drain || tick % 60 == 0 {
        phase(shared, (|| {
            let mut sweep = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.prepare_asset_sweep_for(shared.now(), budget))?;
            sweep.remove_files()?;
            while !sweep.is_empty() { engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.finish_asset_sweep_for(&mut sweep, budget))?; }
            Ok(())
        })(), drain, "the asset sweep")?;
    }
    Ok(work)
}

fn delivery(shared: &Shared, drain: bool) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
    let mut chunks = 0;
    while drain || chunks < 500 {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { break; }
        if phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.run_timeline_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "timeline delivery")?.is_none() { break; }
        chunks += 1;
    }
    Ok(chunks)
}

// @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — ordinary-priority bulk work yields
// outside every shared lock. Aged service protects finite backlogs from traffic.
const BULK_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum BulkKind { Delivery, Recount, Job }
pub(super) struct State {
    next: BulkKind,
    serviced: Instant,
    resume_at: Instant,
    empty_pause: Duration,
    advancing: bool,
    // Explicit mutation-boundary tests pause all background maintenance until drain.
    paused: bool,
    // Separate clock callers (and a dev reset) may wait here; maintenance never takes this lock.
    pub(super) advance: Arc<std::sync::Mutex<()>>,
}
impl Default for State {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let now = Instant::now();
        Self { next: BulkKind::Delivery, serviced: now, resume_at: now, empty_pause: Duration::ZERO,
            advancing: false, paused: false, advance: Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(())) }
    }
}
impl State {
    fn aged(&self, now: Instant) -> bool { now.saturating_duration_since(self.serviced) >= BULK_AGE }
    fn eligible(&self, now: Instant, aged_only: bool) -> bool {
        !self.advancing && !self.paused && if aged_only { self.aged(now) } else { now >= self.resume_at }
    }
    fn completed(&mut self, now: Instant, elapsed: Duration, contended: bool, worked: bool) {
        self.next = match self.next { BulkKind::Delivery => BulkKind::Recount, BulkKind::Recount => BulkKind::Job, BulkKind::Job => BulkKind::Delivery };
        self.serviced = now;
        // Target 1/8 CPU duty under contention, charging consumed thread CPU,
        // including atomic overruns. Cap at 100 ms; aged service is independent.
        let cap = Duration::from_millis(100);
        let charged = if contended { (elapsed * 7).min(cap) } else { Duration::ZERO };
        self.empty_pause += charged;
        // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — empty kinds keep their CPU
        // charge, but repay it with the next useful slice or the empty cycle.
        // Avoid two extra scheduler wakeups for every single delivery slice.
        let pause = if worked || self.empty_pause >= cap { self.empty_pause.min(cap) } else { Duration::ZERO };
        self.empty_pause -= pause;
        // An aged slice can finish after bulk flushes empty-cycle debt. Append
        // this charge without discarding repayment still owed in that ordering.
        self.resume_at = self.resume_at.max(now) + pause;
    }

    fn empty_cycle(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Duration {
        let pause = std::mem::take(&mut self.empty_pause);
        // A timed worker may just have scheduled repayment. Append remaining
        // debt rather than overlapping (and losing) either charge.
        self.resume_at = self.resume_at.max(now) + pause;
        self.resume_at.saturating_duration_since(now)
    }
}

// Admission owns no maintenance gate. Once admitted, the engine serializes the
// cursor's read -> chunk commit -> advance; the gate only protects small state
// accesses. Ordinary work never waits for a bulk thread to enter the engine.
fn bulk_slice(shared: &Shared, aged_only: bool) -> bool {
    if !lock(&shared.maintenance).eligible(Instant::now(), aged_only) { return false; }
    engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| {
        let kind = {
            let state = lock(&shared.maintenance);
            if !state.eligible(Instant::now(), aged_only) { return false; }
            state.next
        };
        let started = SliceClock::start();
        let result = match kind {
            BulkKind::Delivery => engine.run_timeline_turn_for(shared.now(), budget).map(|r| r.is_some()),
            BulkKind::Recount => engine.run_view_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget).map(|r| r.is_some()),
            BulkKind::Job => {
                let run = engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget);
                for line in engine.take_native_logs() { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
                run.map(|run| {
                    if let Some(run) = &run {
                        match run.state {
                            snapback4_core::JobState::Done => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} done: {} chunks, {} rows written", run.name, run.chunks, run.rows_written),
                            snapback4_core::JobState::Parked => {
                                if shared.personas { shared.refusals.job(run); }
                                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} parked after {} chunks: {}", run.name, run.chunks, run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
                            }
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                    run.is_some()
                })
            }
        };
        lock(&shared.maintenance).completed(Instant::now(), started.consumed(), budget == CONTENDED_TIME || shared.engine.has_waiters(), result.as_ref().map_or(true, |worked| *worked));
        phase(shared, result, false, "bulk maintenance").unwrap_or(false)
    })
}

fn timely_engine_work<T: Default>(shared: &Shared, run: impl FnOnce(&mut snapback4_core::Engine, Duration) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
    let result = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| {
        if { let state = lock(&shared.maintenance); state.advancing || state.paused } { return Ok(T::default()); }
        run(engine, budget)
    });
    promote_aged(shared, Instant::now());
    result
}

fn timely(shared: &Shared, tick: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.sweep_expired_for(shared.now(), 2_000, budget)), false, "the expiry sweep")?;
    if !shared.hold_schedules {
        let count = timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, _| Ok(engine.schema().schedules.len()))?;
        for index in 0..count {
            // One atomic boundary per admission, never the entire schedule set.
            // Adoption between slices is harmless: the next tick visits its set.
            let fired = phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_at(shared.now(), index)), false, "schedules")?;

import type { Mutation, Write } from "./contract.js";

export type WitnessReason = "not-ready" | "returned-early" | "event" | "arguments";

type Operations = Record<string, unknown>;
export function registerWebWitness(app: App, api?: Operations): void {
  const handlerClaim = Symbol("witness explicit handler claim");
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.location?.search !== "string") return;
  const enabled = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("witness") === "web";

      const finish = (write: unknown) => {
        if (settled) return;

      };

          clearTimeout(clickTimer);
          clickTimer = setTimeout(() => { click = undefined; submit = undefined; }, 0);
        }
      };

        if (app.mutate === appWrapper) app.mutate = originalApp;

        });

            app.mutate(op, args, { ...options, [handlerClaim]: handlerToken })) as App["mutate"],
          done: () => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); },
        };
      };

    },

      if (!operation) throw Error("E_WITNESS_MUTATION: no matching durable mutation; pass --mutation with a generated API name (ephemeral signals do not prove stored writes)");
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: the app has no signed-in client");

      let settle: ((write: unknown) => void) | undefined;
      const stop = app.client.onWrite(write => { if (write.id === id) { observed = write; settle?.(write); } });
      try {
        const write = await app.mutate(operation as Mutation<any, any>, args, { id, waitSeconds: 0 });
        if (write.state !== "pending") return write;
        return observed ?? await new Promise(resolve => { settle = resolve; });

}

/** Run an event handler with an explicit write claim that survives awaited uploads.
 * @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11 — only explicit per-arm custody survives an async handler.
 * Call synchronously from the clicked control. Use the supplied mutate inside
 * the callback; it carries only that arm's token, never another handler's writes.
 */
export async function witnessForm<T>(app: App, event: Event | { nativeEvent: Event }, handler: (mutate: App["mutate"]) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const native = "nativeEvent" in event ? event.nativeEvent : event;
  const bridge = typeof window === "undefined" ? undefined : (window as unknown as { __snapback4Witness?: {
    claimHandler(owner: App, event: Event): { mutate: App["mutate"]; done(): void } | undefined;
  } }).__snapback4Witness;
  const claim = bridge?.claimHandler(app, native);
  try { return await handler(claim?.mutate ?? app.mutate.bind(app)); }
  finally { claim?.done(); }
}

const substringLocators = new WeakMap<Locator, { locator: Locator; scoped: boolean }>();
/** Match the smallest visible element whose composed descendant text matches.
 * Strings normalize whitespace and stay exact by default; regexes keep their
 * own semantics. Editable values never count. Independent matches remain
 * ambiguous, including repeated rows. */
/** @throws None at construction; locator observation can refuse ambiguous or invalid selectors. */
export function renderedText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp, options: { within?: Locator; exact?: boolean } = {}): Locator {
  const root = options.within ?? page;
  const match = (exact: boolean) => {
    const escaped = typeof text === "string" ? text.trim().split(/\s+/).map(word => word.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")).join("\\s+") : "";
    const pattern = typeof text === "string" ? new RegExp(exact ? `^\\s*${escaped}\\s*$` : escaped, exact ? "" : "i") : text;
    const elements = ":not(input):not(textarea):not(script):not(style):visible";
    const descendants = page.locator(elements).filter({ hasText: pattern });
    return root.locator(elements).filter({ hasText: pattern }).filter({ hasNot: descendants });
  };
  const locator = match(options.exact ?? true);
  if (typeof text === "string" && options.exact !== false) substringLocators.set(locator, { locator: match(false), scoped: options.within !== undefined });
  return locator;
}
/** Activate before taking a screenshot, particularly on loaded shared hosts. */
/** @throws page closed or browser activation failed. */
export async function frontPage(page: Page): Promise<Page> { await page.bringToFront(); return page; }
export { logsDir } from "./proof-process.ts";
/** Returns the fresh nonempty text as a string, after it differs from previous. */
/** @throws observation deadline with scoped locator, last text and visible headings; selector ambiguity is propagated. */
export async function waitForFresh(locator: Locator, previous: string | null, options: number | { within?: Locator; timeoutMs?: number } = 30_000): Promise<string> {
  const timeoutMs = deadline(typeof options === "number" ? options : options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  let substring = substringLocators.get(locator);
  if (typeof options !== "number" && options.within) {
    locator = options.within.locator(locator);
    if (substring) substring = { locator: options.within.locator(substring.locator), scoped: true };
  }
  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let count = 0, text: string | null = null, detail = "";
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    try {
      count = await bounded(locator.count(), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator count");
      text = count ? await bounded(locator.textContent({ timeout: Math.max(1, Math.min(1000, end - performance.now())) }), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator text") : null;
      if (text && text !== previous) return text;
    } catch (error) {
      detail = error instanceof Error && (error.name === "TimeoutError" || /timed out after/.test(error.message))
        ? "locator observation exceeded the remaining proof deadline" : String(error);
      // A strictness/selector error needs its original repair, not a timeout.
      if (error instanceof Error && error.name !== "TimeoutError" && !/timed out after/.test(error.message)) throw error;
    }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
  }
  const hint = count === 0 && substring?.scoped && await bounded(substring.locator.count(), 1000, "substring count").catch(() => 0)
    ? "; a substring match exists; pass a RegExp or `exact: false`" : "";
  const headings = await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => locator.page().locator('h1:visible, h2:visible, [role="heading"]:visible').allTextContents()), 1000, "visible headings").catch(() => []);
  const heading = headings.map(text => text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3).join(" | ").slice(0, 300);
  throw Error(`no fresh signal after ${timeoutMs} ms: ${String(locator)}; last count=${count}, text=${JSON.stringify(text)}; ${count ? "present but unchanged" : "absent"}; previous=${JSON.stringify(previous)}; visible heading=${JSON.stringify(heading || "(none)")}${hint}${detail ? `; ${detail}` : ""}`);
}

}

/** A configuration refusal is an observable app, before any identity or storage
 * is opened. Keeping its client fenced also prevents accidental public reads. */
function refusedApp(options: AppOptions, why: Refusal): App {
  const raw = sdkOwned(() => createClient({ url: options.url, readOnly: options.readOnly ?? false,
    fetch: async () => Response.json({ denied: why }, { status: 403 }) }));
  const client = guardClient(raw, async () => false, () => false, () => why).client;
  bindSessionRefusal(client, () => why);
  const result = { ok: false, why } as const;
  const snapshot: AuthSnapshot = { state: "signed-out", viewer: null, why };
  const refused = () => Promise.resolve(result);
  const auth: AppAuth = { ...snapshot, getSnapshot: () => snapshot, onChange: () => () => {},
    signup: refused, login: refused, guest: refused, logout: refused, logoutEverywhere: refused };
  return { client, online: onlineView(client), onOnlineChange: () => () => {}, auth,
    ready: Promise.resolve(result), mutate: client.mutate, close: () => client.close() };
}

export function createApp(options: AppOptions): App {
  const app = createAppInner(options);
  // The proof driver installs this callback before page scripts. Ordinary
  // applications neither import the witness nor publish an app global.
  if (typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.location?.search === "string" && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("witness") === "web") {
    const install = (window as unknown as Record<symbol, unknown>)[Symbol.for("snapback4.proof.app")];
    if (typeof install === "function") install(app);
  }
  return app;
}

function createAppInner(options: AppOptions): App {
  validateReadOptions(options);

            }
            Ok(())
        })(), false, "the asset sweep")?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn maintain(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
    let mut tick = 0;
    let mut last_tick = Instant::now();
    loop {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
        {
            let state = lock(&shared.maintenance);
            if !state.advancing && !state.paused && let Err(error) = shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now()) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: ephemeral sweep: {error}"); }
        }
        if last_tick.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(1) {
            tick += 1; last_tick = Instant::now();
            // One bounded reclamation slice; abandoned bytes remain charged
            // until their actual records are dropped. No engine lock is held.
            lock(&shared.following).reclaim(shared.now(), snapback4_core::Ceilings { heap_bytes: 1 << 20, ..Default::default() });
            if let Err(error) = timely(&shared, tick) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: maintenance failed: {error}"); }
        }
        promote_aged(&shared, Instant::now());

type Operations = Record<string, unknown>;
export function registerWebWitness(app: App, api?: Operations): void {
  const handlerClaim = Symbol("witness explicit handler claim");
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.location?.search !== "string") return;
  const enabled = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("witness") === "web";
  const armed = () => { if (!enabled) throw Object.assign(Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: add &witness=web"), { code: "E_WITNESS_DISABLED" }); };
  const operations: unknown[] = [];
  const walk = (value: Operations) => { for (const entry of Object.values(value)) {
    if (entry && typeof entry === "object") {
      if ("kind" in entry && entry.kind === "mutation" && !("ephemeral" in entry && entry.ephemeral === true)) operations.push(entry);
      else if (!("kind" in entry)) walk(entry as Operations);

    ready: () => app.ready,
    viewer: () => app.client?.viewer(),

      armed();

    },

      armed();

      form?.cancel();
      const client = app.client, originalClient = client.mutate, originalApp = app.mutate;
      let click: Event | undefined, submit: Event | undefined, clickTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined, claimed = false, cancelObserver = () => {};
      let clickedReady = false, handlerClaimed = false;
      const handlerToken = Symbol("witness handler claim");
      const outsideClick = Symbol("witness outside clicked dispatch");
      let resolve!: (write: unknown) => void;
      const terminal = new Promise(answer => { resolve = answer; });
      let settled = false, noWriteTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      const noWrite = () => finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_NO_WRITE", reason: clickedReady ? "returned-early" : "not-ready", message: "armed click produced no durable mutation (form handler returned early, or the app was not ready)" } });
      const finish = (write: unknown) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true; clearTimeout(expiry); clearTimeout(noWriteTimer); restore(); cancelObserver(); resolve(write);
      };
      // Bound even an abandoned arm or a claimed write that never settles.
      const receiptExpired = () => finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_SENT", message: "claimed write did not settle within 30000 ms; inspect its original intent id" } });
      let expiry = setTimeout(noWrite, 30_000);
      // Chromium's active event ties admission to this actual control's handler.
      // Timers, input autosaves and previously-started writes cannot claim it.
      const capture = (event: Event) => {
        if (handlerClaimed) return; // A later click cannot restart the held handler timer.
        if (!event.isTrusted) { click = undefined; submit = undefined; clearTimeout(clickTimer); return; }

          click = event; submit = undefined; clickedReady = ready;
          clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
          noWriteTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); }, 1000);
          clearTimeout(clickTimer);
          clickTimer = setTimeout(() => { click = undefined; submit = undefined; }, 0);
        }
      };
      const captureSubmit = (event: Event) => {
        if (click && event.isTrusted && (event as SubmitEvent).submitter === button) submit = event;
      };
      document.addEventListener("click", capture, true);
      document.addEventListener("submit", captureSubmit, true);
      const restore = () => {

        if (app.mutate === appWrapper) app.mutate = originalApp;

        document.removeEventListener("submit", captureSubmit, true); clearTimeout(clickTimer);
      };
      const canonical = (value: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(canonical) : value && typeof value === "object"
        ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map(key => [key, canonical((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key])])) : value;
      const execute = async (original: Function, receiver: unknown, op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any): Promise<Write> => {
        const durable = api ? operations.some(value => (value as Mutation<any, any>).name === op.name) : op.kind === "mutation" && (op as { ephemeral?: boolean }).ephemeral !== true;
        const eventMatches = options?.[handlerClaim] !== undefined

          // turn a pending notification into terminal proof evidence either.
          if ((write as Write).state === "pending") return;
          if (id === undefined) { early.push(write); if (early.length > 256) early.shift(); }

//! Bounded, read-only evidence from the store's own commit clock.
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

const SKIPPED_PREVIEW: usize = 32;
const TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 5_000;
const RECENT_COMMITS: usize = 64;
const TABLE_BYTES: usize = 4096;
const TABLE_NAMES: usize = 8;
const REPORT_ERROR_BYTES: usize = 1024;
// Reserve one MiB for the outer clock/work envelope inside the host encoder.
// The reply retains `schedules` and its fired/held partition.
const REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET: usize = (crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING - (1 << 20)) / 2;

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — inspect a fixed recent commit window, never application row values.
pub fn ahead(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Result<Option<String>, RuntimeError> {
    // Reverse primary-key traversal: no full scan, sort, row payloads or retained
    // historical stamp set. octet_length(column) uses SQLite record metadata;
    // unlike a text length/cast/substr, it need not load oversized payloads.
    let mut lead = 0;
    let mut tables = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut omitted = false;
    engine.store().visit_recent_commit_clock(now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS), TABLE_BYTES as i64, RECENT_COMMITS as i64, |record| {
        let at = record.at;
        if at <= now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS) { return Ok(()); }
        lead = lead.max(at.saturating_sub(now));
        let length = record.length.ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("commit clock record is missing touched length".into()))?;
        if length > TABLE_BYTES as i64 { omitted = true; return Ok(()); }
        let bytes = record.touched.ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("commit clock record is missing touched bytes".into()))?;
        match serde_json::from_slice::<Vec<String>>(&bytes) {
            Ok(names) => for name in names {
                if tables.contains(&name) { continue; }
                if tables.len() < TABLE_NAMES && name.len() <= 128 { tables.insert(name); }
                else { omitted = true; }
            },
            Err(_) => omitted = true,
        }
        Ok(())
    })?;
    if lead == 0 { return Ok(None); }
    let mut names = tables.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if omitted { names.push("additional tables omitted".into()); }
    if names.is_empty() { names.push("no table names recorded".into()); }
    Ok(Some(format!(
        "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD: recent commit stamps lead the current clock by up to {lead}ms (latest {RECENT_COMMITS} commits; tables: {}); use a fresh store per offset test ({}), or restart with the same --clock-offset (or dev --resume-clock) to continue",
        names.join(", "), crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())))
}

/// UTC calendar text for Unix milliseconds: schedule boundaries are UTC.
pub fn utc(ms: i64) -> String {
    let (days, rem) = (ms.div_euclid(86_400_000), ms.rem_euclid(86_400_000));
    // Civil-from-days (proleptic Gregorian), exact for the I53 range.
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z - era * 146_097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1_460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let day = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let year = yoe + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    format!("{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}Z", rem / 3_600_000, rem % 3_600_000 / 60_000, rem % 60_000 / 1_000)
}

/// A positive duration in the units `clock advance` accepts, rounded up to a second.
pub fn span(ms: i64) -> String {
    let seconds = (ms.max(0) + 999) / 1_000;
    let (d, h, m, s) = (seconds / 86_400, seconds % 86_400 / 3_600, seconds % 3_600 / 60, seconds % 60);
    let parts: Vec<String> = [(d, "d"), (h, "h"), (m, "m"), (s, "s")].into_iter().filter(|(n, _)| *n > 0).map(|(n, unit)| format!("{n}{unit}")).collect();
    if parts.is_empty() { "0s".into() } else { parts.join("") }
}

/// Each schedule's saved boundary, in declaration order.
pub fn saved_boundaries(engine: &Engine) -> Vec<Option<i64>> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter()
        .map(|schedule| {
            let shape = engine.store().schedule_shape(&schedule.name).ok().flatten();
            if shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|shape| *shape != snapback4_core::value::digest(schedule)) { return None; }
            engine.store().schedule_boundary(&schedule.name).ok().flatten()
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Preflight is bounded before the clock or worker leases change.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — only explicit execution enumerates all crossed windows; coalesced reports keep a bounded preview.
pub fn crossed_boundaries(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Result<Vec<(usize, i64)>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    let mut boundaries = Vec::new();
    let mut report_bytes = 0usize;
    for (index, schedule) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate() {
        // JSON may escape every error byte; count that bound per schedule.
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add(2048 + 6 * (REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3) + 8 * (schedule.name.len() + schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule report exceeds its byte budget; reduce the declared schedules")); }
        if held { continue; }
        let Some(old) = before.get(index).copied().flatten() else { continue; };
        let end = schedule.boundary(now);
        if old >= end { continue; }
        let periods = schedule.periods_between(old, end);
        if each_boundary && periods > (10_000 - boundaries.len()) as i64 {
            return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("--each-boundary crosses more than 10000 schedule windows; use a smaller duration or omit --each-boundary to coalesce"));
        }
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add((if each_boundary { periods as usize } else { (periods as usize).min(SKIPPED_PREVIEW + 1) }).saturating_mul(1024 + 8 * schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule window report exceeds its byte budget; use a smaller duration")); }
        if !each_boundary { continue; }
        let mut boundary = schedule.next(old);
        while boundary <= end {
            boundaries.push((index, boundary));
            boundary = schedule.next(boundary);
        }
    }
    boundaries.sort_by_key(|(index, at)| (*at, *index));
    Ok(boundaries)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — no silent successful advance behind retained schedule markers.
pub fn refuse_held(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64) -> Result<(), snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    for (schedule, saved) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(before) {
        if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| *saved >= schedule.boundary(now) && *saved > schedule.boundary(snapback4_core::now_ms())) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_HELD", "clock", format!(
                "schedule {} already processed boundary {}; requested clock {} has not reached its next window {}. Nothing advanced. Advance {} or use a fresh staging deployment; allowHeld:true explicitly accepts this hold. Never rewind saved boundaries",
                schedule.name, utc(saved), utc(now), utc(due), span(due - now))).guide("clock"));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn period(schedule: &snapback4_core::schema::Schedule) -> String {
    schedule.describe()
}

/// What each schedule did on one clock advance, and why one did not fire.
/// The core fires each schedule at most once per advance, for the latest
/// boundary, and holds it while its saved boundary is ahead of the clock.
// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — missed boundaries fire once; this only explains them.
pub fn schedule_report(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], fired: &[String], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool, port: Option<u16>) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    let after = saved_boundaries(engine);
    // Read only bounded summaries: job args/cursors and full error payloads
    // are irrelevant to this report and must not be loaded then replicated.
    let runs = job_summaries(engine).unwrap_or_default();
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| {
        let (old, saved) = (before.get(index).copied().flatten(), after.get(index).copied().flatten());
        let current = schedule.boundary(now);
        let next = schedule.next(current);
        let mut entry = serde_json::json!({ "name": schedule.name, "target": schedule.target, "period": period(schedule), "fired": false, "firedWindows": [], "skippedBoundaries": [], "nextBoundary": utc(next) });
        if fired.contains(&schedule.name) {
            let end = saved.unwrap_or(current);
            entry["fired"] = true.into();
            let start = schedule.previous(end);
            let window = |boundary| serde_json::json!({"periodStart": schedule.previous(boundary), "periodEnd": boundary});
            let mut windows = Vec::new();
            if each_boundary {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    let mut at = schedule.next(old);
                    while at <= end { windows.push(window(at)); at = schedule.next(at); }
                }
            } else { windows.push(window(end)); }
            if each_boundary {
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired {} windows: {}", windows.len(), windows.iter().map(|window| format!("{} to {}", utc(window["periodStart"].as_i64().unwrap()), utc(window["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")).into();
            }
            entry["firedWindows"] = windows.into();
            entry["periodStart"] = start.into();
            entry["periodEnd"] = end.into();
            if !each_boundary { entry["reason"] = format!("fired for the period {} to {}", utc(start), utc(end)).into(); }
            if let Some(run) = runs.get(&schedule.target) { entry["run"] = run.clone(); }
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar periods count boundaries, not milliseconds.
            let skipped = if each_boundary { 0 } else { old.map(|old| schedule.periods_between(old, end) - 1).unwrap_or(0) };
            if skipped > 0 {
                entry["skippedPeriods"] = skipped.into();
                let mut windows = Vec::new();
                let mut at = schedule.next(old.unwrap());
                while at < end && windows.len() < SKIPPED_PREVIEW {
                    let mut item = window(at);
                    let mut args = serde_json::Map::new();
                    if let Some(program) = engine.program(&schedule.target) {
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") { args.insert("periodStart".into(), schedule.previous(at).into()); }
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") { args.insert("periodEnd".into(), at.into()); }
                    }
                    item["command"] = format!("snapback4 run {} '{}'{}", schedule.target, serde_json::Value::Object(args), port.map(|port| format!(" --port {port}")).unwrap_or_default()).into();
                    windows.push(item); at = schedule.next(at);
                }
                entry["skippedBoundariesOmitted"] = (skipped - windows.len() as i64).into();
                entry["skippedBoundaries"] = windows.into();
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired once, for the latest period {} to {}; {skipped} earlier period{} this advance crossed (from {}) did not run: missed boundaries fire once. Run {} with periodStart/periodEnd for an earlier period",
                    utc(start), utc(end), if skipped == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, utc(old.unwrap_or(end)), schedule.target).into();
            }
        } else if held {
            entry["holdCode"] = "disabled".into();
            entry["reason"] = "schedules are held (dev --hold-schedules or --replay); a restart without the flag resumes at the next boundary".into();
        } else if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| old.is_some() && (*saved > current || *saved == current && *saved > schedule.boundary(snapback4_core::now_ms()))) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            entry["holdCode"] = "saved-boundary".into();
            entry["savedBoundary"] = saved.into();
            entry["heldUntil"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["nextBoundary"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("held by the store: boundary {} was already processed and this clock {} has not reached its next window (restarting resets the process offset, not the saved boundary). One fire per boundary. Advance {} more, or use a fresh staging deployment/store for an independent test; keep the same --clock-offset when continuing a test across serve restarts",
                utc(saved), utc(now), span(due - now)).into();
        } else if old != saved {
            entry["holdCode"] = "initialized".into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("{} started at boundary {}: a schedule fires at the boundary after the first one it sees, next {} ({} from now)",
                if old.is_none() { "this schedule" } else { "this changed schedule" }, utc(saved.unwrap_or(current)), utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        } else {
            entry["holdCode"] = "not-due".into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("next boundary {} ({} from now; schedule times are UTC)", utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        }
        entry
    }).collect()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — every declared schedule has one reported outcome.
pub fn outcomes(schedules: &[serde_json::Value]) -> (Vec<serde_json::Value>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {
    schedules.iter().cloned().partition(|schedule| schedule["fired"] == true)
}

/// The report never loads full error/argument payloads from retained jobs.
fn job_summaries(engine: &Engine) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut summaries = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for summary in engine.store().job_summaries(REPORT_ERROR_BYTES)? {
        let name = summary.name;
        if summaries.contains_key(&name) { continue; }
        let mut error = summary.error;
        let truncated = summary.error_truncated;
        if let Some(text) = &mut error && truncated {
            let mut end = REPORT_ERROR_BYTES.min(text.len());
            while !text.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
            text.truncate(end); text.push('…');
        }
        summaries.insert(name, serde_json::json!({ "id":summary.id, "state":summary.state,
            "rowsWritten":summary.rows_written, "error":error, "errorTruncated":truncated }));
    }
    Ok(summaries)
}

/// Startup lines for schedules the store holds: their saved boundary leads the clock.
pub fn held_schedules(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Vec<String> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(saved_boundaries(engine)).filter_map(|(schedule, saved)| {
        let saved = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > schedule.boundary(now))?;
        let due = schedule.next(saved);
        Some(format!("clock     schedule {} is held until {} ({} from now): its saved boundary {} is ahead of this clock; `clock advance {}` or restart with dev --resume-clock",
            schedule.name, utc(due), span(due - now), utc(saved), span(due - now)))
    }).collect()
}

/// The dev clock offset that continues a store's own time: its newest
/// recent commit stamp (or saved schedule boundary) less wall time, never negative.
pub fn resume_offset(store: &snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore, wall: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    let commit = store.recent_commit_max_at(RECENT_COMMITS as i64)?;
    let boundary = store.latest_schedule_boundary()?;
    let latest = commit.into_iter().chain(boundary).max().unwrap_or(wall);
    // Whole seconds, rounded up, so restarting twice does not drift backwards.
    Ok((latest.saturating_sub(wall).max(0) + 999) / 1_000 * 1_000)
}

pub fn has_data(engine: &Engine) -> bool {
    engine.store().inspection_has_data().unwrap_or(false)
}

pub fn offset_line(offset: i64, running: bool) -> String {
    format!("clock     offset from wall time: {offset:+}ms{}", if running { " (lasts until restart)" } else { " (no running dev; inspecting against wall time)" })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn engine() -> Engine {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table alpha:\n  at: time\n  public\ntable beta:\n  at: time\n  public\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program), 100_000).unwrap()
    }
    fn seed(engine: &mut Engine, table: &str, commit_at: i64, row_at: i64) {
        engine.seed_at([(table.into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
            ("id", snapback4_core::Value::Id(snapback4_core::ids::mint(row_at, &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).unwrap())),
            ("at", snapback4_core::Value::Int(row_at)),
        ]))], commit_at).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_bounds_one_large_job_error_repeated_across_many_schedules() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..500 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        assert_eq!(crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap().len(), 500);
        let fired = engine.fire_schedules(60_000).unwrap();
        let message = "🧪\n\\".repeat(30_000);
        (match engine.store() { snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::Sqlite(inner) => inner }).test_support_conn().execute("UPDATE _jobs SET error = ?1 WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)", [&message]).unwrap();
        let report = schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, 60_000, false, false, None);
        assert_eq!(report.len(), 500);
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["errorTruncated"] == true));
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["error"].as_str().unwrap().len() <= REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3));
        assert!(serde_json::to_vec(&report).unwrap().len() < 2 << 20);
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_refuses_before_the_host_encoder_limit() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..3000 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let refusal = crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_CLOCK_INPUT");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("byte budget"));
        assert_eq!(before, saved_boundaries(&engine));
        assert!(REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET < crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING);
    }
    #[test]
    fn utc_text_and_spans_match_the_calendar() {
        assert_eq!(utc(0), "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(951_782_400_000), "2000-02-29T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(-1), "1969-12-31T23:59:59Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(1_791_468_000_000), "2026-10-08T14:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(span(12_633_000), "3h30m33s");
        assert_eq!(span(86_400_001), "1d1s");
        assert_eq!(span(0), "0s");
    }
    #[test]
    fn resume_offset_follows_the_newest_commit_and_is_never_negative() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 0);
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 3_700_500, 1);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 3_601_000);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 9_000_000).unwrap(), 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn commit_tolerance_and_largest_lead_ignore_future_application_values() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 100_000, 1_000_000);
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 105_000, 2_000_000);
        seed(&mut engine, "beta", 107_000, 3_000_000);
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7000ms (latest 64 commits; tables: beta)"), "{note}");
        let note = ahead(&engine, 99_999).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7001ms (latest 64 commits; tables: alpha, beta)"), "{note}");
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 102_000).unwrap().is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn recent_window_does_not_claim_older_commits_or_read_row_payloads() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 200_000, 200_000);
        for n in 0..RECENT_COMMITS { seed(&mut engine, "beta", 100_000, 300_000 + n as i64); }
        // Deliberately unreadable images: metadata inspection must not deserialize them.
        (match engine.store() { snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::Sqlite(inner) => inner }).test_support_conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = 'not json'", []).unwrap();
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        (match engine.store() { snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::Sqlite(inner) => inner }).test_support_conn().execute("UPDATE _commits SET at = 200000, touched = ?1 WHERE seq = (SELECT MAX(seq) FROM _commits)", ["x".repeat(TABLE_BYTES + 1)]).unwrap();
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("100000ms") && note.contains("additional tables omitted"), "{note}");
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — bounded candidate scan, never an automatic script rewrite.
// Project-specific migration advice. Read authored files only; never print their contents.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, lstatSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, renameSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { previousVersion, compareVersions } from './release-changes.js';
const excluded = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', '.snapback4', 'generated', 'dist', 'build', 'target', 'deploy', 'logs', 'store', 'output', '.runtime']);
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — transition-specific advice and durable nonterminal delivery.
export function upgradeWarnings(root, declarations, previous, current) {
  if (current && (!previous || compareVersions(previous, current) >= 0)) return [];
  const applies = introduced => !current || (compareVersions(previous, introduced) < 0 && compareVersions(current, introduced) >= 0);
  const doors = [], persistence = [], dataJson = [], partialSessions = [], numericAdvance = [];
  let scanned = 0, incomplete = false;
  const walk = (dir, prefix = '', depth = 0) => {
    if (scanned > 10000 || depth > 8) { incomplete = true; return; }
    let entries;
    try { entries = readdirSync(dir, {withFileTypes: true}); } catch { incomplete = true; return; }
    for (const entry of entries.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
      if (++scanned > 10000) { incomplete = true; return; }
      if (excluded.has(entry.name) || entry.name.startsWith('.')) continue;
      const relative = prefix + entry.name, path = join(dir, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) { walk(path, relative + '/', depth + 1); continue; }
      if (!entry.isFile() || !/\.(?:[cm]?[jt]sx?|sh|json)$/.test(entry.name)) continue;
      let size; try { size = lstatSync(path).size; } catch { incomplete = true; continue; }
      if (size > 1048576) { incomplete = true; continue; }
      let source; try { source = readFileSync(path, 'utf8'); } catch { incomplete = true; continue; }
      // Suppress explicit refusal assertions near a door reference, rather
      // than excluding whole test files (which can also rely on dev doors).
      const activeDoors = [...source.matchAll(/\/seed\b|\/dev\/|x-snapback-persona/g)].some(match => {
        const nearby = source.slice(Math.max(0, match.index - 150), match.index + 350);
        return !(/\bassert\.|\bexpect\s*\(/.test(nearby) && /(?:\b403\b|\b401\b|refus|denied|CLOSED)/i.test(nearby));
      });
      if (activeDoors) doors.push(relative);
      if ([...source.matchAll(/\bstartDev\s*\(([^)]*)\)/g)].some(match => !/["']--memory["']/.test(match[1]))) persistence.push(relative);
      if (/\bsession\s*:\s*\{/.test(source)) partialSessions.push(relative);
      if (/\badvance\s*\(/.test(source) && /(?:\bnew\s+Date\s*\(|\bassert\.|[<>+*-])/.test(source)) numericAdvance.push(relative);
      // Candidate scan: shell commands, argv arrays and npm-script strings.
      // Never infer how the parsed result is used or rewrite application code.
      const commandText = source.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
      if (/(?:\bsnapback4(?:@[^\s"']+)?\s+data\b|["']data(?:["']|\s))[^;]{0,2000}--json/.test(commandText)
        || /(?:\bsnapback4\b[^;]{0,2000}--json[^;]{0,2000}\bdata\b|["']--json["'][^;]{0,2000}["']data["'])/.test(commandText)) dataJson.push(relative);
    }
  };
  walk(root);
  const warnings = [];
  const passwordOnly = /^\s*use[ \t]+identity[ \t]+password\b/m.test(declarations) && !/^\s*use[ \t]+identity(?:[ \t]*(?:--[^\n]*)?)?$/m.test(declarations);
  if (passwordOnly && doors.length && applies("0.4.33")) warnings.push('password-only dev doors are CLOSED since 0.4.33: /seed, /dev/* and persona headers refuse. Use deploy staging --staging plus serve staging for real users (guide staging); local fixtures explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors.' + (doors.length ? ' Review: ' + doors.join(', ') : ' No matching dev-door scripts found.'));
  if (persistence.length && applies("0.4.33")) warnings.push('startDev() persists since 0.4.33: reruns see old rows and share the dev store. For disposable acceptance use startDev({args:["--memory"]}); keep persistence for continuity. Review (check each call for explicit memory): ' + persistence.join(', '));
  if (dataJson.length && applies("0.4.35")) warnings.push('data --json returns {rows, more, limit} since 0.4.35, replacing the bare array. One-line fix: JSON.parse(stdout).rows (was JSON.parse(stdout)); use more/limit to detect truncation. Review candidate consumers: ' + dataJson.join(', '));
  if (partialSessions.length && applies("0.4.37")) warnings.push('session literals: complete sessions use validated fields and managed refresh; legacy partial online sessions use only the bearer. One-line explicit fix: createClient({ url, token }); use the complete login/signup session for managed refresh. Review candidates: ' + partialSessions.join(', '));
  if (numericAdvance.length && applies("0.4.36")) warnings.push('advance() returns {now, fired, held}; numeric coercion remains supported. Prefer result.now and inspect held boundaries. Review numeric candidates: ' + numericAdvance.join(', '));
  if (incomplete) warnings.push('migration scan incomplete (unreadable/large files or depth/file limit); inspect remaining authored scripts for /seed, /dev/*, persona headers, startDev() and data --json array consumers.');
  return warnings;
}

/** Delivery receipts are advisory and never block installation or compilation. */
export function acknowledgeWarnings(root, version) {
  const receipt = join(root, '.snapback4/project-warnings-version.json');
  const temporary = `${receipt}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  try {
    mkdirSync(dirname(receipt), {recursive:true});
    writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify({version}) + '\n', {flag:'wx'});
    renameSync(temporary, receipt);
  } finally { try { rmSync(temporary, {force:true}); } catch { /* Advisory only. */ } }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — the OLD CLI invokes the NEW wrapper for its post-upgrade check.
export function firstCheckWarnings(cwd = process.cwd()) {
  if (process.env.SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_BASELINE === '1') return;
  try {
    let root = cwd;
    while (!existsSync(join(root, 'package.json'))) { const parent = dirname(root); if (parent === root) return; root = parent; }
    const schema = join(root, 'snapback');
    if (!existsSync(schema)) return;
    const version = JSON.parse(readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url)), 'utf8')).version;
    const receipt = join(root, '.snapback4/project-warnings-version.json');
    try { if (JSON.parse(readFileSync(receipt, 'utf8')).version === version) return; } catch { /* First check or unreadable receipt: deliver. */ }
    const declarations = readdirSync(schema).filter(name => name.endsWith('.q')).map(name => readFileSync(join(schema, name), 'utf8')).join('\n');
    const warnings = upgradeWarnings(root, declarations, previousVersion(root), version);
    const name = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8')).name ?? root;
    for (const warning of warnings) process.stderr.write(`project ${name}: upgrade warning: ${warning}\n`);
    // Successful writes to stderr count as delivery in terminals and CI.
    // Baseline checks are suppressed above; never repeat on every piped check.
    acknowledgeWarnings(root, version);
  } catch {
    process.stderr.write('snapback4: project migration advice unavailable or not acknowledged; check will continue. Review guide changes and run upgrade to scan authored scripts.\n');
  }
